Partial-write recovery covers the two repaints whose whole intended content is in hand -- the primary band's frame, and a repaint of the alternate plane a question already owns -- each with one fixed cleanup and one whole rebuild of the same repaint in the same call, never a replay of its bytes, and no approval readiness until a later write the session verifies on its own; every other `Partial` -- a plane transition, a document append or carry, a reset's scrollback handoff, the theme query, the retint -- is contained and fatal -- the session ends and leaves its give-up diagnostic -- and its bytes are never replayed (the table under this one gives each path and its reason), though a `Rejected`/`ZeroProgress` write -- an append or a refused handoff alike -- is retained and reoffered under the existing budget rather than dropped. A released binary has none of it (`.prd/03-tui-port.md` item 21; [`.prd/06-qa-harness.md`](../.prd/06-qa-harness.md) rows 3c, 3d and 3e drive the containment and both recoveries on a real pseudoterminal with the fault build). Without it -- and for any other value of it, for every subcommand, and whenever either end is not a terminal -- xfx is the line-oriented shell of the `interactive` row and changes no terminal state. **Widths a terminal disputes, in an answer and in the composer.** The painter counts a scalar a terminal was measured drawing wider than the width table counted it, where fx counts it wider too, as fx counts it (3,838 of them, `src/tui/wrap.rs`'s `FX_COUNTED`; the same cells as fx's in `p5-ctrlo-wider-inline`). Regional indicators are counted as fx counts them, which is not as the terminals draw them: a pair that is no flag four cells (drawn two), a lone one two (one on tmux), and a run a grapheme cluster at a time where fx pairs it along the line (Z U S six, fx four). A document row ends early and loses nothing; where the grid rewrites only the cells that changed, a cell the count put elsewhere can stay on the screen (`ab` of an erased `🇦🇦abcd` in the composer, fx's and xfx's alike) -- the ledger's cause `ri-run-pairing`, RI-COUNT's. The 43 fx and xfx count alike and a terminal draws wider (`src/tui/drawn_wider.rs`) are **named in an answer's rows** -- prose, a code panel and its label, a table's cells, operational text; a base with a skin-tone modifier is named past the base and its modifier stays (`\u{261d}🏻`, whole on both terminals); display only, the journal and `/copy`'s reply keep the model's bytes -- where fx writes them as themselves and its rows lose glyphs on tmux 3.7b and libghostty-vt 1.3.2 (`answer-drawn-wider-named`, unsafe-to-copy; `p5-ctrlo-wider-inline-fam`, `.prd/tui-phase5/receipts/ctrlo-wider/`). **The draft is written as fx writes it** and loses glyphs as fx's does (`y a` of `fam ☝ ⸺ 🖐 x` `U+E0002` `y a` `U+FE0F` `b` on tmux, four `U+261D` in front costing its tail): the user's own text, a decision the ledger's cause `composer-drawn-wider` records, owned by COMPOSER-WIDER (display-only naming with a caret map; a file name an `@` completion puts in the draft is the route a file's text could take, PLAUSIBLE). The card of a sent prompt is the user's text too, written as it stands as a document row, where every glyph stays (fx's card loses `y a` to the anchor after `a` `U+FE0F`: `inline-band-row-anchor`). |
 | full transcript (`C-o`) | ui | partial | Upstream's Ctrl-O screen (`src/ui/full_transcript_screen.zig`, keys `src/core/app/input_full_transcript_runtime.zig:147-198`), on the alternate buffer through the same take-and-give-back the large-change review uses, so the document is exactly as it was when it closes. Every entry the document was given -- a prompt's card, the answer as the markdown presenter completed it (so a notice that lands while it holds a line is an entry of its own before that line, which follows whole, `store.zig:1343-1390`), a notice, a tool call's row in its group (the group's header and every call's row **word-wrapped under their tree rail, never cut**: a continuation is `│ ` under a `├` row and two blanks under the header and the last `└` row, painted in the default colour before the row's own attributes and colour are reopened from the tracked state, a wide character too wide for a one-column row a `?`; fx v0.0.16 `materializeExpandedOverride`/`wrapFullToolStatus`, `tool_group_projection.zig:1061-1100@481104fd`, `transcript_blocks.zig:904-975@481104fd`. A change's lines are written **as fx v0.0.16 writes them, byte for byte** (9db199e4, `appendTerminalSafeLine`, `text_utils.zig:419-442@481104fd`, called at `diff.zig:1273@481104fd`; T-U6b): each is read back from the permission boundary's spelling (`bounded_diff_side`) and spelled again in fx's forms -- a C0 control or `DEL` as `\\x1b`, a C1 control and each mark, separator, embedding, override, isolate and byte-order mark fx names (`isNonPrintingCodepoint`, `:703-708`) as `\\u{009b}` (four lower-case hex digits at least), a line's trailing carriage return dropped, a tab kept (its cells written blank and unstyled, as the terminal leaves the cells fx's tab moves over), a backslash as itself -- so no byte of the file a terminal acts on reaches it;
